Method: 通过分析和组合现有定义，提出统一的可解释性定义；构建结构化可解释性需求并生成分类体系（taxonomy）；基于分类体系分析，将解释的"goodness"（解释质量）和正确性纳入统一定义。

Result: 提出了统一的可解释性定义和结构化需求，形成了可解释性分类体系，并将解释正确性融入定义体系，为开发（自）可解释系统提供指导。

Conclusion: 该研究支持（自）可解释系统形式化标准的制定，统一的分类体系可作为迈向可解释系统认证或审计的有利步骤。

Abstract: Autonomous and software-intensive systems have been growing in occurrence, complexity, and assumed responsibility. Due to the high complexity of these systems, properties like transparency and explainability must be a focus of investigation. To date, no universally applicable definition and guide for the development of (self-)explainable systems exists. A need for explainability standards has already been recognized in the EU AI Act and the IEEE Transparency Standard 7001-2021. To address this need, we propose unified definitions in explainability based on an analysis and combination of existing definitions. Additionally, we present structured explainability requirements that are necessary to build (self-)explainable systems. By analysing the resulting taxonomy, we propose the incorporation of explanation goodness and thus correctness of explanations into the unified definitions. With our approach, we support the development of formal standards for (self-)explainable systems. Establishing such a uniform taxonomy also is a beneficial step towards certifying or auditing explainable systems.

</details>


### [129] [DCAS: Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding to Internalize Planning across Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06113)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文发现基于CLI的软件工程智能体模型在OpenHands环境上微调后，部署到其他脚手架时会显著性能下降，并论证这种下降源于微调对训练脚手架特定规划结构的习得。作者提出DCAS（解耦CLI智能体脚手架）框架，通过后端替换拦截层实现跨脚手架评估和规划感知轨迹收集，证明将规划从固定脚手架工件转变为模型可学习能力可以消除跨脚手架性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源社区中的CLI软件工程智能体模型几乎全部使用OpenHands收集的轨迹数据进行微调，导致模型对该脚手架产生过拟合。模型在非训练脚手架上部署时性能显著下降，而未经微调的基座模型没有这种分化，说明差距是微调引起的，与训练脚手架的约定紧密相关。

Method: 提出DCAS框架，一种后端替换拦截层，在不修改脚手架的情况下路由任何CLI脚手架与任何后端模型之间的API流量，支持跨脚手架评估和规划感知轨迹收集。通过受控的规划源干预实验验证规划质量是高杠杆组件，并使用DCAS收集的小规模规划感知轨迹进行微调。

Result: 规划质量干预带来的增益超过观测到的跨脚手架性能下降；在单一脚手架上使用少量DCAS收集的规划感知轨迹微调的模型，在非训练脚手架上获得一致的性能提升；显式规划和隐式规划两种规划类型在训练数据中可经验性分离。

Conclusion: 跨脚手架性能差距是由微调过程习得的脚手架特定规划结构所致，解决办法是将规划从固定的脚手架工件转变为模型可学习能力。DCAS使这一能力成为可能，且小规模规划感知数据即可带来显著的跨脚手架泛化收益。

Abstract: CLI-based software-engineering agents have matured rapidly, yet the open ecosystem has converged on a single training environment: trajectory datasets used to fine-tune open models are collected almost exclusively under OpenHands. Models fine-tuned on this data score well under OpenHands but degrade substantially when deployed under any non-training scaffold. Untrained base models do not show this divergence, indicating the gap is fine-tuning-induced and tied to the conventions of the training scaffold. We argue that a load-bearing scaffold-specific behavior is planning structure, in two senses this paper distinguishes: explicit planning, a pre-execution plan produced as a first-class artifact, and implicit planning, the structural conventions that shape execution throughout the agent loop. Under this hypothesis, closing the gap requires moving planning from a fixed scaffold artifact to a learned model capability. We introduce Decoupling CLI Agent Scaffolding (DCAS), a backend-substitution interception layer that routes API traffic between any CLI scaffold and any backend model without modifying the scaffold, enabling cross-scaffold evaluation and planning-aware trajectory collection. Using DCAS, a controlled plan-source intervention confirms planning quality is a high-leverage component, with gains exceeding the cross-scaffold drops we observe. A model fine-tuned on a small set of DCAS-collected planning-aware trajectories under a single scaffold gains consistently across non-training scaffolds, and the two senses of planning are empirically separable in training data.

</details>


### [130] [Learning Globally Reusable Skills for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06153)
*Chen Yang,Jiashuo Tian,Ziqi Wang,Xinyin Liu,Meiru Ye,Junjie Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了GSE（全局化技能演化框架），通过技能关系图和基于聚类的技能整合，解决LLM代理技能演化中缺乏关联建模和过拟合的问题。在软件工程任务上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理技能演化方法将技能演化视为一系列局部更新，忽略了技能之间的相互关系，且容易产生过拟合的技能更新，导致技能泛化能力差，难以迁移到跨任务场景。

Method: GSE框架包含三个核心组件：1）技能关系图（SRG），显式建模并协同演化技能间的相互关系以保持技能库一致性；2）基于聚类的技能整合，从局部更新中抽象出可复用的能力以提升泛化；3）重放驱动的验证机制，防止过拟合并避免行为退化。

Result: 在bug暴露测试生成和误报bug报告过滤两项软件工程任务上，GSE在OpenHands和mini-SWE-agent两个编码代理上均取得最佳精度、召回率和F1分数。测试生成任务的精度和召回率分别提升6.1%~34.1%和31.8%~180.0%，误报过滤任务分别提升15.4%~96.4%和13.1%~19.8%；工业代理部署中F1分数提升61.4%。

Conclusion: GSE通过全局化的技能演化和关系建模，有效提升了LLM代理技能演化的泛化能力和一致性，在多个代理和任务上展示了显著的性能提升与可推广性。

Abstract: Automated skill evolution enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to continuously improve without expensive retraining. However, existing approaches typically treat skill evolution as a sequence of local updates, overlooking relationships among skills and often producing overfitted skill updates that fail to generalize across tasks. We propose GSE, a globalized skill evolution framework that jointly optimizes skill compatibility and skill generalization. To preserve consistency across the skill bank, GSE maintains a Skill Relation Graph (SRG) that explicitly models and co-evolves inter-skill relationships. To improve generalization, GSE performs cluster-based skill consolidation to abstract reusable capabilities from local updates and employs replay-driven verification to prevent overfitting and behavioral regressions. We evaluate GSE on two representative software engineering tasks: bug-revealing test generation and false-positive bug report filtering. Across two state-of-the-art coding agents, OpenHands and mini-SWE-agent, GSE consistently achieves the best precision, recall, and F1-score. Compared with existing evolution techniques, GSE improves precision and recall by 6.1%~34.1% and 31.8%~180.0% for test generation, and by 15.4%~96.4% and 13.1%~19.8% for false-positive filtering. Deployment on an internal industrial agent further yields a 61.4% improvement in F1-score, demonstrating the effectiveness and generalizability of GSE for evolving effective skills.

</details>


### [131] [Predicting Agile Success: The Critical Few Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06228)
*Ridewaan Hanslo,Maureen Tanner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文采用实证主义方法，通过PLS-SEM分析识别敏捷软件开发项目的关键成功因素（CSF），并构建了一个可用于评估和衡量项目成功的新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管敏捷项目比传统软件项目管理方法更成功，但其整体成功率仍然较低，大量项目仍面临挑战或失败。这种低成功率归因于项目未针对敏捷项目的关键成功因素和当代项目成功标准进行严格评估。

Method: 采用实证主义研究范式，从敏捷从业者视角调查敏捷软件开发项目的CSF；使用PLS-SEM（基于SmartPLS工具）分析数据并进行假设检验，以识别各构念与项目成功标准之间的显著关系。

Result: 研究发现少数关键因素对敏捷项目成功具有显著贡献，并开发了一个新颖的模型，可用于评估和衡量项目成功。

Conclusion: 该研究构建的CSF模型为敏捷项目成功评估提供了理论和实证基础，有助于提升敏捷软件开发项目的成功率。

Abstract: While Agile projects are more successful than traditional software development project management approaches, their overall success rate remains relatively low, with a high percentage of projects still deemed challenged or failed. This low project success rate is attributed to projects not being rigorously evaluated against critical success factors (CSF) of Agile projects and contemporary project success criteria. To identify the CSF that contribute to Agile software development project success as perceived by Agile practitioners, this study used a positivist approach to investigate the CSF of Agile software development projects. PLS-SEM with SmartPLS was used to analyse the data and test the hypotheses to identify significant relationships between the constructs and project success criteria. This research found that a few critical factors significantly contribute to Agile project success. The study developed a novel model that can be used to evaluate and measure project success.

</details>


### [132] [Automatic Translation of Unstructured Requirements into Linear Temporal Logic through Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06287)
*Alexandra Newcomb,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种现代通用大语言模型（LLMs）在无需任务特定微调的情况下，通过少样本提示策略将非结构化自然语言需求自动转换为线性时序逻辑（LTL）公式的能力，并基于15条异构需求生成450条候选公式进行人工语义评估、pass@k和自一致性度量，结果表明LLMs在该任务上能达到实际可用性能，有望成为半自动化形式化工作流的前端助手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键和任务关键系统的验证依赖于数学上精确的形式化规格，而将非结构化自然语言需求自动翻译为形式化规格仍是一个挑战，传统方法在这方面存在瓶颈。

Method: 通过少样本提示策略，对六种当代通用LLMs在15条结构各异的基准需求上进行测试；对每个需求-模型对收集5次独立生成，共得到450条候选LTL公式；通过人工语义评估、pass@k（k=1,3,5）以及衡量跨随机试验的句法可复现性的自一致性度量来评估性能。

Result: 结果表明，当前通用LLMs无需任务特定微调即可在非结构化自然语言到LTL的转换任务上取得实际可用水平的性能；研究还通过生成的自然语言解释和基于时间线的LTL可视化探讨了非专家用户的易理解性问题。

Conclusion: 现代LLMs正逐渐成为半自动化形式化工作流中可行的前端助手，有潜力在需求工程与形式化方法之间架起桥梁。

Abstract: Automatically translating unstructured natural language requirements into formal specifications remains a challenge in requirements engineering and formal methods, particularly for safety- and mission-critical systems whose verification depends on mathematically precise specifications. This paper evaluates whether contemporary off-the-shelf Large Language Models (LLMs) can help bridge this gap by generating Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) formulas directly from unstructured requirements. The study examines six modern LLMs using a few-shot prompting strategy on a heterogeneous benchmark of 15 structurally varied requirements. Five independent generations were collected for each requirement-model pair, yielding 450 candidate LTL formulas in total. Performance was assessed through manual semantic evaluation, pass@k for k in {1, 3, 5}, and a self-consistency measure capturing syntactic reproducibility across stochastic trials. The results indicate that current general-purpose LLMs can achieve practically significant performance on the unstructured NL-to-LTL task without task-specific fine-tuning. The study also considers understandability for non-experts by pairing generated formulas with model-produced natural language explanations and discussing the complementary use of timeline-based LTL visualization. The findings suggest that modern LLMs are becoming viable front-end assistants for semi-automated formalization workflows.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [133] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MS-MLB（多发性硬化机器学习基准），一个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据的开放可复现基准，用于MS与健康对照的分类任务，采用泄漏控制的统一评估流程，涵盖嵌套交叉验证、保留测试集、置信区间、ROC/PR分析、校准测量和MS研究评分。在保留集上，梯度提升以MS研究评分93.83、AUC-ROC 0.989、敏感性0.950、特异性0.778、F1 0.927和Brier分数0.050排名第一。该基准是首个从GSE17048全血RNA数据中聚焦MS与健康对照分类并内置外部模型提交路径的开放基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化（MS）的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI、实验室证据和排除其他解释，过程复杂。全血RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关免疫信号，但缺乏一个可复现、泄漏受控的开放基准来公平评估机器学习方法在该任务上的性能。

Method: 基于公开GSE17048队列数据，构建MS与健康对照的二分类任务；在统一且泄漏控制的流水线下评估多种机器学习算法，包括嵌套交叉验证、独立的保留测试集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC和精确率-召回率分析、校准测量，并提出探索性MS研究评分用于综合排序。基准框架内置外部模型提交流程。

Result: 在保留测试集上，梯度提升算法排名第一，MS研究评分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989，敏感性0.950，特异性0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个基于GSE17048全血RNA表达数据、聚焦MS与健康对照分类、并内置外部模型提交路径的开放基准。该评分仅用于研究比较，未经临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [134] [Potential Matching Optimal Transport: Continuous Normalizing Flows for Exact $p$-Wasserstein Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05666)
*Lishuo Zhang,Ruizhi Huang,Yang Yu,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT)，一个用于一般p-代价最优传输的势流框架。PMOT用标量势参数化CNF速度场（广义Benamou-Brenier形式），通过自诱导匹配损失训练势梯度，并实现灵活的终端分布匹配。论文证明了零损失精确性定理，在合成基准上PMOT学习到的p特定映射与p匹配的OT参考一致，在高维表格数据上作为基于似然的密度模型表现出竞争力，并通过MMD颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最优传输框架通常局限于特定的代价函数或难以扩展到大维空间，需要一种能够处理一般p-代价最优传输的通用框架，同时保持可扩展性和灵活的终端匹配能力。

Method: PMOT将连续归一化流(CNF)的速度场参数化为标量势的梯度，采用广义Benamou-Brenier形式，针对选定的指数p。训练过程使用自诱导匹配损失，沿由模型自身端点确定的直线桥进行匹配，同时允许灵活的终端分布匹配。主要定理证明在正则性、精确终端匹配和唯一性假设下，零损失解满足广义Benamou-Brenier最优性系统，并能恢复p-最优传输映射和动力学。

Result: 在合成基准上，PMOT学习的p特定映射与对应的p匹配OT参考一致。在高维表格数据上作为基于似然的密度模型具有竞争力。MMD-based颜色变换实验展示了灵活的基于样本的终端匹配能力。

Conclusion: PMOT为一般p-代价OT提供了一种统一的势流框架，兼具理论保证（零损失精确性）和实际性能（合成基准、密度建模和样本匹配实验均验证了其有效性）。

Abstract: We introduce Potential Matching Optimal Transport (PMOT), a potential-flow framework for general $p$-cost optimal transport with $c_p(x,y)=\|x-y\|^p$. PMOT parameterizes the CNF velocity field with a scalar potential in the generalized Benamou--Brenier form for the chosen exponent $p$. It trains the potential gradient with a self-induced matching loss along straight bridges determined by the model's own endpoints, while allowing flexible terminal distribution matching. Our main result establishes zero-loss exactness: under the stated regularity, exact terminal matching, and uniqueness assumptions, any zero-loss solution satisfies the generalized Benamou--Brenier optimality system and recovers the corresponding $p$-optimal transport map and dynamics. On synthetic benchmarks, PMOT learns $p$-specific maps that agree with the corresponding $p$-matched OT references. It also remains competitive as a likelihood-based density model on high-dimensional tabular data, and an MMD-based color transformation experiment demonstrates flexible sample-based terminal matching.

</details>


### [135] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了CRAFTER框架，通过挖掘冻结预测模型的残差中的可解释修正特征，实现轻量级的事后校正。该框架结合组合搜索和LLM两种特征生成器，并通过验证集门控筛选特征，在六个数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上超越了所有专门的自动特征工程系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测模型在结构化、重复性的方式上经常失败，而微调修复成本高昂。作者提出研究"修正特征发现"这一新范式，从模型故障过程中挖掘可解释特征，而非传统的数据生成过程，以低成本驱动事后的轻量级校正器。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网络冻结，通过两种互补的生成器挖掘残差：(1) 对原始输入通道进行组合搜索；(2) 使用LLM提出命名特征组合、二元标志和短小可执行代码。所有候选特征（无论来源）都经过单一验证集门控接受或拒绝，最终由验证集选择的校正器应用被接受的特征或保持预测不变。该源码无关的流程允许先前特征工程系统在相同条件下评估，从而将预测改进归因于特征来源本身。

Result: 在六个公共数据集和六个冻结骨干网络上，CRAFTER在每一个特征预算下都超越了所有专门的自动特征工程系统，将校正器单独实现的改进约翻倍，并将最弱骨干网络的误差最多降低27%。这些收益在不同LLM后端下表现稳健，且在微调后的骨干网络上仍然保持。

Conclusion: CRAFTER验证了以模型故障过程为建模对象的修正特征发现范式的有效性。通过将特征来源与验证门控解耦，该系统不仅提供了卓越的预测校正性能，还成为一个可归因的工具，使预测改进可被追溯到特征来源，揭示了修正特征发现优于传统数据生成特征工程的优势。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [136] [Alternating Levenberg-Marquardt Training of Physics-Informed Neural Networks with Fourier-Enhanced Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05892)
*Yulun Wu,Matthieu Barreau,Miguel Aguiar,Karl H. Johansson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALM-PINN，一种将表示学习与系数拟合解耦的优化框架，通过傅里叶增强基函数丰富潜空间高频成分，并采用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解非线性最小二乘问题，在高频和多尺度PDE求解中比现有方法降低多达两个数量级的相对L²误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在求解具有高频或多尺度解的PDE以及强非线性问题时往往效果不佳，其根本原因在于两个因素：谱偏置（神经网络倾向于欠拟合高频特征）和表示-系数耦合（表示学习与系数拟合纠缠在单一非凸优化目标中）。现有方法难以同时解决这两个问题。

Method: FALM-PINN采用双层优化框架：上层问题学习一个傅里叶增强基函数，通过向潜空间注入高频成分以缓解谱偏置；下层问题在该基上拟合投影系数，使用Levenberg-Marquardt算法求解非线性最小二乘问题以解耦表示学习与系数拟合。该方法适用于一般非线性和耦合PDE系统，对线性PDE可退化为一阶凸优化问题。作者证明了两种情况下交替训练方案的全局收敛性。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的高频和非线性PDE数值实验中，FALM-PINN相较于最先进的基线方法，相对L²误差降低了多达两个数量级。

Conclusion: FALM-PINN通过解耦表示学习与系数拟合、引入傅里叶增强基函数，有效克服了PINNs在求解高频/多尺度及强非线性PDE时的谱偏置与耦合问题，同时具备全局收敛保证，代表了PINNs优化框架的重要进展。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) often fail to accurately resolve partial differential equations (PDEs) with high-frequency or multi-scale solutions, as well as strongly nonlinear problems. Two factors underlie this difficulty: spectral bias, the tendency of neural networks to underfit high-frequency features; and representation-coefficient coupling, the entanglement of representation learning and coefficient fitting within a single nonconvex optimization objective. In this work, we propose the Fourier-enhanced alternating Levenberg--Marquardt PINN (FALM-PINN), an optimization framework that decouples representation learning from coefficient fitting. The upper-level problem learns a Fourier-enhanced basis that enriches the latent space with high-frequency components, while the lower-level problem resolves the coupling by fitting the projection coefficients on this basis, solving a nonlinear least-squares problem with the Levenberg--Marquardt algorithm. The framework applies to general nonlinear and coupled PDE systems, and reduces to a single-step convex optimization problem for linear PDEs. We prove global convergence of the alternating training scheme in both cases. Numerical examples on multiple challenging high-frequency and nonlinear PDEs show that FALM-PINN achieves relative $L^2$ errors up to two orders of magnitude lower than state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [137] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率性编程语言，用于构建基于LLM的流程，使开发者能够量化并传播整个应用流程中的不确定性，并在不修改流程代码的情况下实验不同的推理缩放技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM输出缺乏准确性和置信度度量，在涉及多次LLM调用和其他工具的复杂流程中，这种不确定性会被累积放大，导致开发者和终端用户难以信任结果。现有方法缺乏系统性地量化和传播不确定性的手段。

Method: 提出一种概率性编程语言，专门用于编写LLM-based流程。该语言允许开发者量化流程中每一步的不确定性并沿流程传播，同时支持在不更改流程逻辑代码的前提下，灵活实验不同的推理缩放（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究证明了该语言的能力，并进行了案例研究，构建了一个面向Rocq定理证明器的定理证明agent。

Conclusion: 概率性编程语言为LLM-based应用的可靠性提供了系统化的不确定性建模与传播方案，降低了开发者在构建可信LLM应用时的门槛，并已在定理证明等复杂任务上得到验证。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [138] [Kastor: An efficient fine-tuning strategy for generative emulation of PDE simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06107)
*Guillaume Couairon,Alexis Jacq,Yu-Han Wu,Renu Singh,Yana Hasson,Quentin Berthet,Romuald Elie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Kastor方法，将确定性物理基础模型改造为高效精准的生成式替代模型，通过两阶段推理、均值预测正则化（MPR）和空间梯度匹配三大组件，在模拟预测中显著降低误差累积与计算成本，并在The Well基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDE求解器计算昂贵，而标准自回归ML模拟器在长时程预测中存在误差累积问题，且难以捕捉复杂物理系统的随机性。亟需一种既能大幅降低计算成本、又能保持长时程精度和物理真实性的生成式替代模型。

Method: Kastor包含三个核心组件：(1) 两阶段推理方案：采用大步长因果自回归模型结合非因果时间超分辨率网络，在减少误差累积的同时最小化计算开销；(2) 均值预测正则化（MPR）：一种新的训练目标，约束生成模型在零噪声条件下预测确定性分布均值，提升FGN和扩散模拟器的性能与稳定性；(3) 空间梯度匹配：引入梯度约束以提高模拟精度和物理保真度（以功率谱密度衡量）。

Result: 在The Well基准的多种模拟数据集上，Kastor在预测精度、频谱一致性和计算效率方面均优于对比方法。相比基于Walrus微调方法论的参考模型，平均预测误差降低42.9%，且在10个数据集中有8个在方差归一化RMSE（VRMSE）指标上超越Walrus。

Conclusion: 通过两阶段推理、均值预测正则化和空间梯度匹配的组合，Kastor成功将确定性物理基础模型转化为兼具高效性与高精度的生成式模拟器，有效克服了自回归模型误差累积和随机性建模不足的问题，为物理仿真加速提供了有力方案。

Abstract: Machine learning offers a promising avenue to accelerate physical simulations by replacing computationally expensive traditional Partial Differential Equation (PDE) solvers with fast, differentiable surrogate models. However, standard auto-regressive ML emulators often suffer from error accumulation over long horizons and struggle to capture the stochasticity of complex physical systems. In this paper, we propose Kastor, a comprehensive methodology to adapt a deterministic physics foundation model into a highly efficient and accurate generative surrogate. First, we introduce a two-stage inference scheme that combines a large-stride causal auto-regressive model with a non-causal temporal super-resolution network, significantly reducing error accumulation while minimizing computational cost. Second, we present Mean prediction regularization (MPR), a novel training objective that constrains the generative model to predict the deterministic distribution mean under null noise conditioning. This regularization dramatically improves the performance and stability of both Functional Generative Networks (FGN) and diffusion-based emulators. Finally, we demonstrate that incorporating spatial gradient matching improves the accuracy and physical fidelity of the simulations as measured by power spectrum density. Extensive evaluations on diverse simulation datasets of the benchmark The Well show that with these components, our model outperforms competing methods in forecasting accuracy, spectral consistency, and computational efficiency. Our model achieves a 42.9% average reduction in forecasting compared to our reference based on the Walrus finetuning methodology, and outperforms Walrus for 8 out of 10 datasets on variance-normalized RMSE (VRMSE).

</details>


### [139] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列语言模型。通过将感知与描述解耦（确定性代码计算统计量，LLM只负责语言表达），解决了多模态时间序列-语言对齐训练中的"自监督陷阱"和三难困境（可靠、真实、可扩展不可兼得）。CGTime在多变量理解任务上超越GPT-4o-mini和GPT-5.4-nano等更大模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法训练多模态模型将时间序列与语言对齐时存在两个核心问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——用LLM描述序列来训练模型，标签质量受限于LLM自身的感知能力，数据永远无法教会模型超过标注者已知的内容；(2) 大多数数据集仅用单变量，但关键模式（跨通道相关、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只出现在多变量场景中，而这恰恰暴露了标签LLM的局限。这导致现有方法在可靠、真实、可扩展三方面无法兼得。

Method: 将感知与描述解耦：采用确定性代码从真实开源多变量序列中计算一组统计特征，再由LLM将这些预计算的事实进行言语化/自然语言表达。感知任务（LLM不擅长）由计算完成，LLM只负责表达任务。由此构建CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列语言模型。

Result: 在保留的基准测试上，CGTime在多变量事实得分上达到最佳（0.283），优于GPT-4o-mini（0.173）和GPT-5.4-nano（0.203），且与所有基线的差异通过Holm校正的配对显著性检验。此外，CGTime在生成字幕中陈述可验证数值事实更准确，涵盖更广泛的统计属性范围。

Conclusion: 通过计算接地的方式将感知从描述中解耦，可以用小得多的模型在多变量时间序列理解上超越远大规模的通⽤模型，证明了"感知交给确定性计算、表达交给语言模型"这一范式在时间序列-语言对齐任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [140] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种解耦空间推理范式（DiSR），将3D感知与推理明确分离：使用现成的专家感知模型重建结构化3D证据，再通过LoRA微调LLM仅基于显式几何证据进行推理。无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具策略，DiSR即可在主流空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，并具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端方法通过大规模训练隐式地联合获取3D感知和推理能力，作者观察到现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而LLM擅长组合符号推理，两者存在互补优势，因此提出显式分离感知与推理的替代范式。

Method: DiSR包含两个关键步骤：1）利用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；2）使用LoRA对LLM进行参数高效微调，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理。框架简单且无需复杂工具使用策略。

Result: 在没有大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具策略的条件下，DiSR在流行的空间推理基准上达到了具有竞争力的性能，同时展现出更强的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是可扩展且有效的空间智能替代范式，优于端到端建模方式。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [141] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中常见的做法——将风格潜变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验——并不能保证风格表示与类别标签独立。作者通过理论分解和大量实验证明，仅匹配边缘分布对类条件分布没有任何约束，导致潜风格仍高度携带类别信息。实验显示多个基线模型在全局MMD接近零的情况下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签。作者提出了四种缓解策略，并验证了仅基于边缘分布的散度无法认证类别独立性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型（如VAE、GAN等）广泛应用高斯先验约束风格潜变量z_s，并将其边缘分布匹配到固定高斯视为风格与类别独立的证据。作者指出这一解释是错误的——边缘分布的匹配无法约束类条件分布，从而无法保证真正的因子化解耦。论文旨在纠正这一常见误解，并给出理论框架和实证证据。

Method: 作者推导了一个精确分解，证明边缘分布匹配只是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一。实验中构建了一个案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线，测量全局MMD与线性探针准确率之间的关系；在六种不同扰动（模型容量、课程学习、先验几何、监督信号、两个数据集）下验证泄漏的持续性；测试四种缓解策略（将线性探针准确率降至21%-46%）；并提出两种后处理方案——条件先验和实证风格库（风格银行）以改善外部评估的类条件生成。

Result: 所有基线模型在近零全局MMD下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%恢复类别标签（10%为随机水平）；案例模型达到99.15%的聚类准确率，而外部评估的类条件生成成功率仅16%。泄漏在六种扰动下持续存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖仍然存在。后验条件先验在MNIST上使外部评估类条件生成达到0.97（无重训练），CIFAR-10上仅0.41；实证风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 仅基于风格潜变量边缘分布计算的任何散度（如MMD）都无法证明其与类别标签的独立性。仅报告边缘统计量不足以验证因子化生成模型中所声称的性质。消除边缘分布与类条件分布的失配是必要的但不是充分的因子化条件。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [142] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM框架，通过四阶段数据合成流程（persona-task对、前缀引导规则集、质量过滤rubric、结构化验证轨迹）训练多模态大模型作为'执行者'而非'生成者'来理解并遵循分级rubric指令。仅用10K合成样本，将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且泛化到四种额外开源MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态指令通常捆绑多个重要性不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据将指令遵循简化为回答单一自包含问题。作者研究这一差距，提出'rubric理解'概念，将模型定位为遵循rubric的执行者而非对照rubric衡量的生成者。

Method: 提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：(1)生成persona-task配对；(2)生成前缀引导的规则集；(3)质量过滤rubric；(4)生成结构化验证轨迹。同时提出PRISM-Eval评测基准，使用Loose和Strict两种确定性匹配指标，不需要推理时的评判模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，同时保持通用基准的平均性能；增益可迁移至四种额外开源MLLM（涵盖dense和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化的rubric监督是通往多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令遵循的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一种参数高效的参数高效多任务SFT框架，将过度拟合感知的多任务SFT状态重构为紧凑可合并的适配器状态，冻结预训练骨干网络，只训练注入的适配器，在任务级峰值回滚适配器检查点。相比msft，AuroSFT在五个骨干网络上取得更高平均准确率（61.36% vs 59.85%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务监督微调（SFT）常将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务达到最佳泛化的时间点不同。已有方法msft通过任务级回放、排除和回滚来解决这一问题，但其原始公式将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署上代价高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余活跃混合上训练。层级别上，每个适配器采用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层，作用于低秩权重因子而非样本表示，使得更新在输入上保持线性、秩有界，且能精确合并进冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干网络的对比协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达到61.36%，高于对应msft参考行的59.85%，并且在全部五个骨干网络上均获得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过参数高效的适配器状态实现了过度拟合感知的多任务SFT的紧凑、可合并的状态表示，在提升性能的同时大幅降低了阶段转换的存储和部署成本。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [144] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroOFT在量化正交微调(QOFT)基础上附加零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，突破了原方法仅限线性正交变换的限制，在保持初始化时与QOFT功能等价的前提下，通过RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间和自适应非线性基实现输入依赖的非线性校正，在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置下较Matched QOFT提升1.30-2.70% Macro-6，较QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，同时节省32.3-44.7%可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOFT虽然实现了低比特语言模型的参数高效适配，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换内，无法进行输入依赖的非线性校正，这限制了模型的表达能力和适应性能。

Method: AuroOFT保持QOFT作为稳定的量化兼容分支，在每层适配的线性层上附加一个零起始门控低秩非线性残差。它使用RMS归一化将激活映射到紧凑潜空间，采用带边界或token依赖门控的自适应非线性基。零初始化上投影确保AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT功能完全一致，而正交性保持为分支级稳定性属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在Qwen2.5 1.5B/3B设置上较Matched QOFT的Macro-6提升1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA 6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模上相对QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。小型选择题式数学集仅作为协议敏感性诊断。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过引入非线性残差分支成功突破了QOFT的线性正交约束，在提升性能的同时保持了参数效率优势，代码已在匿名仓库公开。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [145] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的个性化税收感知投资管理应用：基于FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘，用户用自然语言描述投资目标，系统路由到六个投资任务之一，通过三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、混合专家分配策略、个性化LoRA适配器）生成与券商实时集成的投资组合建议。系统已功能完整并通过端到端集成测试（Alpaca纸面交易），但尚未对外开放给真实用户，属于部署前应用。作者提供了14天滚动回测（含自助置信区间）作为初步验证，并总结了几条工程经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户所享有的个性化、税收感知的投资组合管理服务。现有机器人投顾使用静态规则分配，而机构级系统对个人投资者设有账户门槛和技术壁垒。作者旨在弥合这一差距，构建一个功能完整、可实际部署的个性化投资管理应用。

Method: 系统架构包括：(1) FastAPI后端与Web仪表盘，支持自然语言投资目标描述；(2) 将目标路由到六个投资任务之一；(3) 三阶段强化学习系统：自监督跨资产编码器、带学习意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）分配策略，以及从个人券商行为中个性化推荐而无需重训共享模型的轻量LoRA适配器。系统还包含多用户认证、预览后再确认的投资流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计的操作完整性链，并针对Alpaca纸面交易API进行了端到端集成测试。

Result: 系统功能完整并通过了与实时券商API（Alpaca纸面交易模式）的全链路集成测试。作者提供了14天walk-forward回测（含bootstrapped置信区间）作为部署前的初步验证。同时报告了几条工程经验：静默失效的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据。

Conclusion: 这是一个诚实标注的部署前（pre-deployment）新兴应用，具备通往完整部署的具体路径。其工程设计经验对基于外部实时数据源构建应用强化学习系统的其他项目具有通用借鉴价值。作者未将回测结果宣传为生产性能，仅作为初步验证。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [146] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型用于野火后泥石流预测，发现TabPFN模型在未增强数据上表现最佳（威胁评分0.637），SHAP分析显示短时降雨强度是最关键特征，且使用TabPFN生成的合成数据增强可提升除CNN外所有模型的性能，深度学习模型平均威胁评分提升最大（+0.041）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对减少社区和基础设施风险至关重要，但存在三个关键挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件特征空间重叠、模型可解释性需求、以及训练数据稀缺。本文旨在通过系统评估机器学习模型来解决这些问题。

Method: 使用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，比较15种机器学习模型（包括TabPFN），采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能，使用SHAP分析特征重要性，并利用TabPFN生成的合成样本进行数据增强。

Result: TabPFN在未增强数据上取得最高威胁评分0.637，超越最佳树模型；SHAP分析显示短时降雨强度和风暴累积量是最重要特征，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小；合成数据增强在所有模型中（除CNN外）均提升性能，深度学习模型平均威胁评分提升最大（+0.041）。

Conclusion: 通过结合严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，本文为改善野火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，表明TabPFN在该任务中具有优越性能，且合成数据增强是缓解数据稀缺问题的有效手段。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [147] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OSPDIM，一种基于在线SPD流形信息最大化的源自由在线无监督域自适应框架，用于解决脑机接口中由标签偏移导致的黎曼几何对齐失效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的BCI系统跨受试者/跨会话泛化需要无监督域自适应（UDA），但现有黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）隐式假设类别先验平衡。在实际在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术在几何上错误对齐目标数据分布。

Method: OSPDIM在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化优化该参数以纠正不平衡数据流导致的几何偏斜。与依赖全局批量统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM实时估计并纠正几何偏置。

Result: 2D SPD矩阵模拟实验直观展示了OSPDIM能成功纠正标准中心化方法无法处理的错位问题。在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM显著优于标准黎曼基线，尤其在严重类别不平衡的在线自适应挑战场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际可插拔的BCI系统提供了鲁棒解决方案，有效处理在线场景下的标签偏移问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [148] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QEvict，一种三级KV缓存管理方案，用可恢复的驱逐取代传统二元保留/删除策略，解决了推理过程中token被永久驱逐后无法重新激活的问题，在固定内存预算下提升了长上下文任务的注意力保留和信息保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归大语言模型推理受限于KV缓存的内存占用。现有工作通过基于注意力分数的驱逐策略减少占用，但这类策略隐含了不可逆的二元决策——token一旦被驱逐便无法恢复。然而在解码过程中，token和窗口的重要性会随生成查询的变化而漂移，导致标准驱逐策略永久丢弃了那些在完整缓存模型下后来会获得大量注意力的状态，从而造成信息损失。

Method: QEvict采用三级KV缓存管理：1）将高置信度窗口以全精度（full precision）保留；2）中间窗口存储在量化可恢复层（quantized recoverable tier）；3）仅删除最低置信度窗口。解码过程中，累积注意力分数动态更新窗口重要性，当量化窗口重新变得重要时会被反量化并提升回全精度层。此外，作者提出了两个诊断指标——Future Missed Mass（衡量被丢弃状态未来获得的注意力）和Global LIR（衡量历史不活跃区域的重新激活），用于刻画驱逐导致的注意力漂移行为。

Result: 在长上下文理解、检索和推理基准测试中，QEvict相对于代表性的驱逐和量化基线方法均取得一致改进，减少了被错过的注意力并提升了信息保持能力。

Conclusion: QEvict通过将不可逆的二元驱逐转变为可恢复的量化降级机制，在固定内存预算下既保留了更广泛的历史上下文，又对最重要区域维持精确的全精度表示，有效解决了解码过程中token重要性漂移带来的信息丢失问题，为KV缓存管理提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive large language model inference is increasingly constrained by the memory footprint of the Key-Value (KV) cache. A dominant line of work reduces this footprint by evicting tokens that appear unimportant under attention-derived scores. However, such policies make an implicit irreversible decision: once a token is evicted, it cannot become useful again. We show that this assumption is brittle during decoding. Token and window importance drift as generated queries evolve, causing standard eviction policies to permanently discard states that later receive substantial attention under the full-cache model. To characterize this behaviour, we introduce Future Missed Mass and Global LIR, two diagnostics that measure future attention assigned to discarded states and the reactivation of historically inactive regions. We propose QEvict, a three-tier KV-cache management scheme that replaces binary retain-or-delete eviction with recoverable eviction. QEvict maintains high-confidence windows in full precision, stores intermediate windows in a quantized recoverable tier, and deletes only the lowest-confidence windows. During decoding, cumulative attention scores update window importance and when a quantized window becomes important again, it is dequantized and promoted to the full-precision. Under a fixed memory budget, this design preserves broader historical context while retaining exact full precision for the most important regions. Across long-context understanding, retrieval, and reasoning benchmarks, QEvict consistently improves over representative eviction and quantization baselines, reducing missed attention and improving information retention

</details>


### [149] [Align-RAG: Alignment Is All You Need for TSFM In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05571)
*Mohammad Asadi,Soheil Hor,Bardiya Akhbari,Jack W. O'Sullivan,Tahoura Nedaee,Layne C. Price,Raviteja Anantha,Euan Ashley,Ehsan Adeli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Align-RAG是一种无训练参数的检索增强预测方法，通过对检索到的过去-未来窗口进行闭式幅度重缩放和整数滞后相移，使冻结的时间序列基础模型（TSFM）无需学习适配器即可动态利用检索上下文。在七个标准数据集上，Align-RAG以平均-3.75%的MSE优于最先进的训练式检索适配器，并进一步提升四种冻结TSFM的零样本性能2.5%-13.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强预测方法通常依赖训练好的融合模块（即适配器）将检索示例合并到主干模型的预测中，其隐含假设是冻结的TSFM无法自主动态结合检索上下文。作者希望验证这一假设是否必要，并探索是否可以在不训练任何参数的情况下实现等价的性能提升。

Method: 提出Align-RAG，一种完全无需训练的方法。核心思想是在将检索到的过去-未来窗口送入冻结主干模型的上下文之前，对其应用闭式（closed-form）的逐对幅度重缩放（per-pair amplitude rescaling）和整数滞后相移（integer-lag phase shift）。无任何学习参数。同时通过对比冻结主干的预测偏移与闭式岭回归预测偏移来探究对齐起作用的原因，并采用未来洗牌（future-shuffle）对照实验排除"未来平均"解释。

Result: 在全部七个标准基准数据集上，Align-RAG优于最先进的训练式检索适配器（冻结Chronos-Bolt上平均MSE降低3.75%）。在四种不同架构的额外冻结TSFM上，零样本MSE提升2.5%-13.7%，且无需逐主干调参。消融与对照实验表明，对齐示例诱导的预测偏移与闭式岭回归预测器一致，而未来洗牌对照排除了"对单个未来进行平均"的简单解释。

Conclusion: 冻结的TSFM本身已具备动态上下文检索利用能力，无需训练融合模块；闭式对齐应作为检索增强预测中训练任何融合模块之前的默认基线。先前归因于学习式融合的性能提升可以在零训练条件下完全恢复。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented forecasting promises to adapt frozen Time Series Foundation Models (TSFMs) to new domains without fine-tuning, but recent methods typically rely on learned fusion modules, i.e., trained adapters that merge retrieved examples into the backbone's forecast, based on the assumption that frozen backbones cannot dynamically incorporate retrieved context on their own. We show this assumption is unnecessary. We introduce Align-RAG, a training-free method that applies a closed-form per-pair amplitude rescaling and integer-lag phase shift to retrieved past-future windows before they enter a frozen backbone's context. With no learned parameters, Align-RAG outperforms the state-of-the-art trained retrieval adapter on a frozen Chronos-Bolt on all seven datasets of the standard benchmark (avg -3.75% MSE), showing that the gains previously attributed to learned fusion are recoverable without any training. Align-RAG further improves zero-shot MSE on four additional frozen TSFMs with various architectures by 2.5% to 13.7% per backbone with no per-backbone tuning. To probe why alignment helps, we compare the frozen backbone's prediction shift under aligned demonstrations to the closed-form ridge prediction shift on the same pairs. We find that aligned demonstrations induce prediction shifts that track a closed-form ridge predictor on the same pairs, with a future-shuffle control ruling out a futures-averaging account. Together, these results indicate that frozen TSFMs already support dynamic in-context use of retrievals, and that closed-form alignment should be the default baseline for retrieval-augmented forecasting before any fusion module is trained. Code available at: https://github.com/masadi-99/align-rag

</details>


### [150] [DG-FedReuse: Proxy-Gradient-Gated Cached-Update Reuse with Matched Sparse Uplink Accounting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05358)
*Rahil Aftab,Vineet Kumar Rakesh,Soumya Mazumdar,Tapas Samanta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DG-FedReuse，一种联邦学习模拟器级机制，允许选中的客户端在随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新，并在90轮预算下实现83-85%的上行更新数据字段节省（相比Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习反复进行局部优化和模型更新传输，导致大量通信开销。现有方法如Top-K压缩已能节省部分通信成本，但仍存在进一步提升的空间。作者希望利用之前轮次中缓存的更新，通过年龄衰减和阈值控制，在保持精度的同时进一步降低上行通信开销。

Method: DG-FedReuse在模拟器层面实现：(1) 当随机头梯度差异代理低于轮次相关阈值时，允许选中客户端贡献年龄衰减的缓存更新；(2) 设置硬缓存年龄上限和最小新鲜客户端配额来约束重用；(3) 新鲜更新采用自适应每张量Top-K数值字段表示。实验覆盖六个图像分类数据集、50个虚拟客户端、Dirichlet标签异质性（α=0.5）和三重随机种子。

Result: 在90轮公共预算下，DG-FedReuse实现83.36-85.42%的建模更新数据字段上行节省（对比匹配Top-K FedAvg的76.88%），种子对齐精度差异为-5.29至-0.14个百分点。最优测试精度（测试控制检查点）为-2.38至+0.45个百分点。对称稠密模型下行敏感性分析将总节省降至41.68-42.71%，相对Top-K FedAvg的增量收益仅为3.24-4.27个百分点。

Conclusion: DG-FedReuse所提出的重用规则仅在实现的模拟器中得到刻画，研究并不主张其具备无偏泛化、端到端带宽降低、运行时或能量节省、更快收敛或对现有过期更新与懒惰聚合方法的优越性。通信结论高度依赖会计边界，下行链路开销不容忽视。

Abstract: Federated learning repeatedly incurs local optimization and model-update transmission. We study DG-FedReuse, a simulator-level mechanism that allows selected clients to contribute age-decayed cached updates when a stochastic head-gradient discrepancy proxy remains below a round-dependent threshold. A hard cache-age limit and minimum fresh-client quota constrain reuse, while fresh updates use an adaptive per-tensor Top-K numerical-field representation. Experiments cover six image-classification datasets, 50 virtual clients, Dirichlet label heterogeneity (α=0.5), and three seeds. At a common 90-round budget, DG-FedReuse yields 83.36-85.42% modeled update-data-field uplink saving, compared with 76.88% for matched Top-K FedAvg; the seed-aligned accuracy differences range from -5.29 to -0.14 percentage points. Best-observed test accuracies obtained under test-controlled checkpointing are retained only as exploratory archival evidence and range from -2.38 to +0.45 percentage points relative to matched FedAvg. A symmetric dense-model-downlink sensitivity reduces the headline saving to 41.68-F42.71% and the incremental gain over Top-K FedAvg to 3.24-4.27 percentage points, demonstrating the dependence of communication conclusions on the accounting boundary. The study characterizes the proposed reuse rule in the implemented simulator; it does not establish unbiased generalization, end-to-end bandwidth reduction, runtime or energy savings, faster convergence, or superiority over existing stale-update and lazy-aggregation methods.

</details>


### [151] [Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs) for Predictive Latent Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05371)
*Hailong Jiang,Emran Hossain,Feng Yu,Jianfeng Zhu,Guilin Zhang,Wulan Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出量子结构化世界模型（QSWMs），一种受量子理论启发的预测性世界建模框架，使用复数值表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态，并建立三个基础性质（经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧致性），在初等元胞自动机上评估发现复数值QSWM具有局部预测潜力，但在长时程滚动和密度矩阵变体上存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型大多使用经典向量、概率分布、循环隐状态或Transformer激活来表示潜在状态，作者希望探索量子理论启发的数学结构（如复数值表示和密度矩阵式潜在状态）是否能为世界建模提供有效的归纳偏置，从而提升预测、仿真和规划能力。

Method: 引入QSWM框架，包含结构化潜在状态、潜在转移算子和测量启发的解码映射。具体包括：1) 建立三个基础性质：经典包含性、预测充分性和结构化紧致性；2) 实例化复数值和密度矩阵式两种QSWM变体；3) 在初等元胞自动机上进行评估，与强经典基线对比。

Result: 复数值QSWM在局部预测方面展现出有前景的潜力，但研究发现其在长时程滚动预测方面存在局限性，且密度矩阵式变体的表现不如预期。

Conclusion: 量子启发的结构化潜在状态为世界建模提供了新的思路，复数值表示在局部预测上有潜力，但目前框架在长时程预测和密度矩阵变体上仍有不足，需要进一步研究和改进。

Abstract: World models learn latent states that summarize interaction histories, evolve over time, and support prediction, simulation, or planning. Most existing world models represent these states using classical vectors, probability distributions, recurrent hidden states, or transformer activations. In this paper, we introduce Quantum-Structured World Models (QSWMs), a quantum-inspired framework for predictive world modeling with structured latent states, latent transition operators, and measurement-inspired decoding maps. We study whether mathematical structures inspired by quantum theory, such as complex-valued representations and density-matrix-like latents, provide useful inductive biases for world modeling. We establish three foundational properties: classical inclusion, predictive sufficiency, and structured compactness. We then instantiate complex-valued and density-matrix-like QSWM variants and evaluate them on elementary cellular automata against strong classical baselines. Results show promising local predictive potential for complex-valued QSWMs, while also revealing limitations in long-horizon rollout, density-matrix variants

</details>


### [152] [Spectral Distillation: From Nonlinear Dynamics to Linear State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05416)
*Liane Galanti,Devan Shah,Shlomo Fortgang,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏的流水线，从非线性动力系统的观测中学习紧凑的线性状态空间表示，无需直接求解非凸的系统识别问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从非线性动力系统学习线性状态空间表示通常涉及非凸的系统识别问题，难以求解且缺乏理论保证。作者希望找到一个可证明的凸方法替代方案，能够以紧凑的线性模型提取非线性动力学。

Method: 流水线分为两步：首先使用Observation Spectral Filtering（OSF）这一凸方法从非线性系统的观测中学习隐式谱预测器，使其与最优线性观测器竞争；然后通过谱到LDS蒸馏（spectral-to-LDS distillation）将预测器转换为显式的循环线性动力系统。核心定理表明蒸馏后的LDS平均预测误差可分解为指数级小的蒸馏项和由Luenberger复杂度控制的OSF学习项，且保证与维度无关，仅依赖于观测器复杂度而非非线性系统所需的潜在维度。

Result: 在理论上，获得了首个端到端可证明的方法，通过凸学习加可证明蒸馏提取非线性动力学的后验最优LDS表示。在实验上，线性LDS基准测试和MuJoCo行为克隆任务中，train-then-distill流水线生成的紧凑LDS预测器匹配或优于直接训练的基线模型。

Conclusion: 作者证明了非线性动力系统可以通过凸方法加蒸馏的可证明流水线，以紧凑的线性状态空间表示进行学习，其误差保证仅取决于观测器复杂度而非潜在状态维度，为非线性系统识别提供了理论上可靠且实践有效的替代路径。

Abstract: Can nonlinear dynamical systems be learned through a compact linear state-space representation, without directly solving a non-convex system-identification problem? We give a provable pipeline for doing so. Starting from observations of an unknown nonlinear dynamical system, we first learn an implicit spectral predictor using Observation Spectral Filtering (OSF), a convex method that competes with the best linear observer for the system. We then apply spectral-to-LDS distillation to convert this predictor into an explicit recurrent linear dynamical system. Our main theorem shows that the average prediction error of the distilled LDS decomposes into an exponentially-small distillation term and the OSF learning term governed by the Luenberger complexity of the best observer. The guarantee is dimension-free: it depends on observer complexity rather than on the latent dimension needed to represent the nonlinear system. To our knowledge, this yields the first end-to-end provable method for extracting a best-in-hindsight LDS representation of nonlinear dynamics through convex learning followed by provable distillation. Experiments on linear LDS benchmarks and MuJoCo behavior cloning show that the train-then-distill pipeline produces compact LDS predictors that match or outperform directly trained baselines.

</details>


### [153] [Perturbation Sensitivity at Convergence: A Simple Signal for Identifying Spuriously Correlated Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05419)
*Nilesh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需群组标注即可识别受虚假相关性影响的样本的方法。该方法在模型收敛后，通过对输入施加固定扰动，利用"虚假相关样本遵循共享分类规则、而异常样本依赖个体特定配置更脆弱"的特性来区分两类样本。实验表明该方法在Waterbirds上将最差群组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，接近使用真实群组标签的85.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法识别受虚假相关性影响的样本需要群组标注的验证集来选择早期训练干预的epoch超参数，这在实际中难以获得。作者希望找到一种无需群组标注、无需定位早期训练epoch即可识别受影响样本的方法。

Method: 利用模型收敛后的信号：对训练样本输入施加固定扰动，统计预测翻转情况。与虚假相关性一致的样本由共享规则分类，扰动后预测稳定；而其余样本依赖输入特异的配置，对扰动更脆弱。该方法仅需每个样本两次前向传播，无需群组标注和早期停止epoch选择。

Result: 在Waterbirds数据集上，使用检测到的样本进行再平衡训练，将最差群组准确率从57.3%提升至80.8%，而使用真实群组标签的基线达到85.8%。

Conclusion: 收敛后模型对输入扰动的预测翻转频率可作为无需群组标注的可靠信号，用于识别受虚假相关性影响的样本，从而提升最差群组性能，且方法简便（仅需两次前向传播）、无需额外标注或超参数调优。

Abstract: Models trained by empirical risk minimization on data containing spurious correlations achieve high average accuracy while failing on subpopulations where the correlation does not hold. Existing methods for identifying the affected samples without group annotations rely on signals from early training, which requires locating the epoch at which to intervene, a hyperparameter typically selected using group-labeled validation data. We show that a usable signal is available after convergence, when loss no longer distinguishes the two populations. Samples consistent with the spurious correlation are classified by a shared rule, while the remaining samples are fit through configurations specific to individual inputs and are correspondingly more fragile. Applying a fixed perturbation to a converged model's inputs flips the predictions of the latter far more often than the former. The resulting procedure requires two forward passes per training sample, no group annotations at any stage, and no early-stopping epoch. Using the detected samples to rebalance training raises worst-group accuracy on Waterbirds from 57.3% to 80.8%, against 85.8% with ground-truth group labels.

</details>


### [154] [IFlowNets: Extending Generative Samplers to Learn Strategies in Incomplete Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05422)
*Conor M. Artman,Nicholas Di,Scott Perkins*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了信息流网络（IFlowNets），将对抗流网络（AFlowNets）从完整信息博弈扩展到不完全信息博弈，通过放宽原有的生成流网络约束条件，使生成的密度能够对应有效的玩家策略，并在三个标准博弈环境中取得与OSMCCFR和标准RL方法相当或更优的性能和速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多将强化学习（RL）与反事实遗憾（CFR）方法结合以权衡计算速度与性能，但在不完全信息博弈中对生成式采样框架的探索较少。此外，先前针对完整信息博弈建立的生成流网络约束条件在不完全信息博弈中无法获得有效的密度（对应玩家策略）和有效的训练目标。

Method: 作者将AFlowNets扩展至不完全信息博弈，提出信息流网络（IFlowNets）。首先证明了原有AFlowNets的约束条件在不完全信息环境中不适用，然后提出了推广的约束条件与训练目标，使IFlowNets能够正确生成对应玩家策略的密度分布，并且该框架严格推广了AFlowNets。

Result: 在三个标准博弈环境中的初步实验表明，IFlowNets在性能和速度上与Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret（OSMCCFR）算法以及标准RL方法相当或更优。

Conclusion: IFlowNets成功将生成流网络框架推广到不完全信息博弈，解决了原有约束的不可行性问题，并在实验验证中展现出竞争力，为生成式采样在博弈论应用中的进一步探索提供了新方向。

Abstract: While many algorithms blend reinforcement learning (RL) with counterfactual regret (CFR) methods to leverage tradeoffs in computational speed and performance, there are fewer investigations into generative sampling frameworks in game theoretic applications in incomplete information games. We extend a generative flow network framework, Adversarial Flow Networks (AFlowNets), to incomplete information games, called Information Flow Networks (IFNs). We prove that previously established constraints for generative flow networks in complete information games are inadmissible for obtaining valid densities (corresponding to player strategies) and a valid training objective. We show that our proposed generalization, IFlowNets, alleviates this issue and strictly generalizes AFlowNets. In preliminary results for three standard game environments, IFlowNets perform comparably to or better than Outcome Sampling Monte Carlo Counterfactual Regret (OSMCCFR) and standard RL-based methods in performance and speed.

</details>


### [155] [Why the Third Axis Is Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05423)
*Michael Timothy Bennett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示生成式训练中"探索性建模(XM)"的本质是优化"自由"（行为约束的弱度）而非"生成表现力"。作者证明XM的平均损失取决于候选答案错过可接受区域的概率（随K次方上升），实证表明自由选择器在30次对比中29次胜过XM验证集选择，并指出生成表现力只是自由的一种模式计数代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式训练中，单次输出比较会使仅产生常见答案的模型优于保留更广泛行为库的模型。探讨性建模(XM)通过每次比较产生K个输出并更新最接近者，被宣称是"第三预训练轴"与生成表现力相关。作者旨在揭示这个第三轴的真实本质，并检验其在分布偏移下的选择能力。

Method: 理论部分：证明平均XM损失取决于候选答案错过可接受区域的概率，探索将该概率提高到K次方幂；当K>1时，匹配概率随自由上升。实证部分：在Forward XM实验中测试更大K对测得自由的影响；训练XM候选池后，将验证集选择与读取未标注父上下文的自由选择器进行比较。

Result: 理论上证明K>1时匹配概率随自由单调上升；实证中更大K增加或饱和了测得的自由，在上下文依赖目标下每个测试值都增加自由；自由选择器在30个案例中29个胜出。此前研究还表明自由选择比MDL在归纳实验中提高110-500%性能。

Conclusion: XM是手段，自由是目的。生成表现力只是自由的模式计数代理，丢弃了赋予自由泛化意义的扩展结构。直接选择自由比XM验证集选择在分布偏移下表现更好，自由的弱约束性质是其泛化优势的核心。

Abstract: In generative training, a model produces an output and is penalised for its difference from an example. With one output per comparison, a model that produces one common answer can outperform a model retaining a broader repertoire. Explorative Modeling (XM) produces $K$ outputs per comparison and updates on the closest, claiming exploration as a "third pretraining axis" associated with generative expressivity. Here I show the third axis is actually freedom, meaning the weakness of the constraint implied by a model's behaviour. Previous work showed freedom is a property of function rather than form. Parameters, architecture, minimum-description-length (MDL), and data can vary while the behavioural constraint remains unchanged. It was formally proved that weakest models are likeliest to generalise, and freedom selection beat MDL by 110-500\% in induction experiments. I prove average XM loss depends on the chance a candidate misses an acceptable region, with exploration raising miss probability to power $K$. For $K>1$, match probability rises with freedom. I then demonstrate empirically that XM optimises for freedom. In a Forward XM experiment, larger $K$ increased or saturated measured freedom, and increased freedom at every tested value under context-dependent targets. I trained XM candidate pools and compared validation selection with a freedom selector that read unlabelled parent contexts. Freedom won in 29 of 30 cases. Generative expressivity is a mode-count proxy for freedom, that discards the extension structure that gives freedom its generalisation significance. XM is a means, freedom an end, and selecting for freedom improved XM under distribution shift.

</details>


### [156] [EvoHarness-RL: Learning Self-Evolving Runtime Harness for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05446)
*Xuying Ning,Dongqi Fu,Tianxin Wei,Hanqing Zeng,Yuanchen Bei,Bingxuan Li,Zihao Li,Qifan Wang,Xiang Shen,Yifan Wu,Jiayi Liu,Hong Li,Yinglong Xia,Xiangjun Fan,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoHarness-RL，通过离线训练可学习的harness策略，让长时程LLM智能体在运行时在线构建和更新外部harness状态（Belief、Progress、Experience），在ALFWorld上达到96.9%的成功率，并揭示了harness退火和harness演化两种关键动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程LLM智能体越来越多地依赖外部执行支持（如状态维护、进度追踪、工具调用、结果验证和经验复用），但有效使用harness面临两个耦合挑战：从嘈杂交互轨迹中形成状态，以及对外部状态访问的运行时控制。现有方法通常依赖提示、启发式或领域特定约定来手动设计外部工作空间及使用策略，缺乏系统化的学习方法。

Method: 提出了两种学习机制：(1) 监督式harness微调，教会基础智能体harness动作空间及如何构建有用的外部状态；(2) 成本感知的GRPO（组相对策略优化），探索协调策略以在长时程交互中选择性读取、更新和整合状态。将Belief、Progress、Experience (BPE)暴露为策略面向的harness状态，在ALFWorld上使用Qwen3-8B作为基础模型进行实例化。

Result: 在ALFWorld上达到96.9%的成功率。揭示了两种关键动态：harness退火（训练将重复的harness使用模式内化到模型策略中，使智能体从频繁调用harness转向选择性外部状态访问）和harness演化（进度更新和经验整合将harness精炼为紧凑、任务自适应的状态基底）。

Conclusion: 长时程智能体能从可训练的harness策略中获益——即通过训练来构建和协调外部harness工作空间，这比单纯提供更强的工具或更大的记忆更有效。研究结果表明，harness策略学习是提升长时程智能体性能的重要且有前景的方向。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents increasingly rely on external execution support to maintain state, track progress, invoke tools, verify outcomes, and reuse experience across interactions. However, effective harness use raises two coupled challenges: state formation from noisy interaction traces and runtime control over external-state access. Existing agents usually handle both through prompts, heuristics, or domain-specific conventions, leaving the external workspace and its usage policy manually engineered. To address this, we study the problem of harness policy learning, where agents learn harness policies offline and deploy them to construct and update external harness state online during runtime task execution. We introduce EvoHarness-RL, which exposes Belief, Progress, and Experience (BPE) as policy-facing harness state. Supervised harness fine-tuning teaches the base agent the harness action space and how to construct useful external state, while cost-aware GRPO explores coordination policies to selectively read, update, and consolidate that state during long-horizon interaction. Instantiated on ALFWorld with a Qwen3-8B LLM, EvoHarness-RL reaches 96.9% success and reveals two key dynamics: harness annealing, where training internalizes recurring harness-use patterns into the model policy and shifts the agent from frequent harness calls toward selective external-state access, and harness evolution, where progress updates and experience consolidation refine the harness into a compact, task-adaptive state substrate. These results suggest that long-horizon agents benefit from trainable policies for constructing and coordinating with external harness workspaces, beyond simply adding stronger tools or larger memories.

</details>


### [157] [Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotopes for Identifiable and Refinable Predictive Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05454)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合概率Zonotope（HProbZ）作为神经网络预测头，将不确定性分解为离散选择（二元生成器）、有界系统漂移（有界生成器）和随机噪声（随机生成器）三类来源，并以zonotope生成器形式表示，支持闭式似然、跨步骤代数和推理时解析的模式风险与无分布多模态共形集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率预测头通常输出高斯混合或单一共形区域，无法分离真实任务中常见的三类不同来源的不确定性：模式间的离散选择、所选模式内有界的系统性漂移，以及不可约的随机噪声。

Method: 设计HProbZ输出头，将三类不确定性分别建模为zonotope的二元、有界和随机生成器，通过卷积获得闭式似然；将有界生成器在预测步间共享，实现单次前向传播中观察一步即可细化所有剩余步的预测分布；并证明三类生成器在似然下可辨识（至置换），且其密度与有限高斯混合在表示上不同。

Result: 在代表性预测基准上的实证分析表明，HProbZ相对于同编码器混合模型基线具有有效性，同时提供了混合模型或凸共形预测器无法共同提供的结构性质。

Conclusion: HProbZ统一了离散模式选择、有界漂移和随机噪声三类不确定性建模，兼具闭式似然、共享结构带来的跨步推理细化、解析风险估计与多模态共形集，在性能和结构性质上均有优势。

Abstract: Probabilistic prediction heads in neural networks typically output either a Gaussian mixture or a single conformal region. Neither separates the distinct sources of uncertainty often present in real prediction tasks: a discrete choice among modes, bounded systematic drift within the chosen mode, and irreducible stochastic noise. We introduce the Hybrid Probabilistic Zonotope (HProbZ), an output head that represents these three sources as binary, bounded, and stochastic generators of a zonotope, and admits a closed-form likelihood by convolution. Sharing the bounded generator across prediction steps couples future predictions algebraically, so observing one step refines the predictive distribution at every remaining step in a single forward pass. We establish that the three generators are identifiable from the likelihood up to permutation, and that an HProbZ density is representationally distinct from any finite Gaussian mixture. The same shared structure provides analytic per-mode risk and distribution-free multi-modal conformal sets at inference time. Empirical analysis on representative prediction benchmarks supports the effectiveness of the design relative to same-encoder mixture baselines, while offering structural properties that mixture or convex-conformal predictors do not jointly provide.

</details>


### [158] [Matrix Zonotopic Attention: A Context-Adaptive Value Projection for Set Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05472)
*Zhen Zhang,Amr Alanwar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多头注意力中输入相关软路由与输入无关线性值投影的不对称性对置换不变集合目标的影响，提出变换自由度（TDOF）度量，证明了上下文刚性注意力需要与目标TDOF成比例的深度，而单层上下文自适应值族即可表示相同目标。基于此提出矩阵Zonotopic注意力（MZAttn），用上下文自适应的矩阵-zonotope族替代固定值投影，在初始化时退化为标准多头注意力，保持置换等变性，实验结果与TDOF预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多头注意力中软路由是输入依赖的（选择哪些token的value），而值投影是输入独立的（固定线性变换），导致每样本的聚合映射算子对任意输入集都是相同的。这种不对称性限制了注意力在置换不变集合目标上的表达能力。作者旨在揭示这种刚性的理论后果，并提出机制性的解决方案。

Method: 1) 定义TDOF（变换自由度）作为目标算子的复杂度度量，计数精确表示所需的输入依赖方向数；2) 证明深度分离结果：上下文刚性注意力需要与目标TDOF成比例的深度，而单层上下文自适应值族即可表示；3) 提出MZAttn，用中心矩阵加生成矩阵和（由输入依赖门控加权）构成矩阵-zonotope族替代固定值投影，初始化时退化为标准MHA，保持置换等变性，并具有数据驱动的可达性解释。

Result: 实验涵盖多种集合预测任务，结果与TDOF预测一致：架构优势具有选择性——在高秩、稀疏组合式依赖输入集的目标上优势明显，而在聚合统计量类目标上与参数匹配的标准注意力竞争力相当，优势较小。

Conclusion: 注意力的值投影上下文自适应能力是关键表达能力瓶颈。通过在值投影上引入输入依赖的自适应族（矩阵-zonotope构造），单层即可实现原本需要深层堆叠才能达成的表示能力，且这种优势是有选择性的，与任务的TDOF特性相关。

Abstract: Multi-head attention combines an input-dependent softmax routing with an input-independent linear value projection, so the per-sample operator mapping aggregated values to outputs is the same for every input set. We study the consequences of this asymmetry for permutation-invariant set targets. We introduce the Transformation Degrees of Freedom (TDOF) of a target operator, a complexity measure counting the input-dependent directions an exact representation requires, and present a depth-separation analysis showing that context-rigid attention needs depth proportional to the target's TDOF, whereas a single layer with a context-adaptive value family can represent the same target. Building on this analysis, we propose Matrix Zonotopic Attention (MZAttn), which replaces the fixed value projection with a context-adaptive matrix-zonotope family: a centre matrix plus a sum of generator matrices weighted by input-dependent gates. The construction reduces to standard multi-head attention at initialisation, preserves permutation equivariance, and admits a data-driven reachability interpretation. Experiments on a range of set-prediction tasks are consistent with the TDOF prediction that the architectural advantage is selective: it appears on targets that depend on the input set in a high-rank, sparsely combinatorial way, and is small on aggregate-statistic targets where parameter-matched standard attention is already competitive.

</details>


### [159] [KV-Skill: Forging Expertise in the Model's Native Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05475)
*Zhaowei Han,Xiang Zhang,Bing Han,Kai Liu,Danqi Hu,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KV-Skill，一种外部因子化算子的设计空间，通过轻量接口将任务知识以算子形式存入冻结语言模型的外部存储中，既可将文本技能转为算子（Registration），也可直接从任务奖励中学习紧凑隐式算子（Reward learning），在不增加prompt长度的前提下提升任务性能并支持算子的独立加载与移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的任务知识存储方式存在权衡：文本提示模块化但每次使用都需重新解释；权重更新使能力难以独立加载、移除或共享。作者希望探索一种既模块化又独立于backbone的外部算子表达方式。

Method: KV-Skill包含两条互补路径：1) Registration：将文本技能转换为文本派生的算子，并训练一个共享的每-backbone轻量接口；2) Reward learning：直接从任务奖励中学习紧凑的隐式算子（可有或无作者技能辅助）。两种路径均不增加prompt的位置数，冻结的LM通过轻量接口读取外部因子化算子。

Result: 在十个基准和四个来自三个模型家族的backbone上，文本转KV-Skill一致提升了程序性知识的有效性。在Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath上，Registration达到77.2准确率，远超源文本技能的23.4、SkillOpt的52.0和SoftSkill的64.5。在匹配的奖励训练与参数预算下，KV-Skill在八个匹配设置中的七个上优于soft prefix、prefix tuning和LoRA。秩分析显示文本派生算子每个注入层仅用一个任务对齐方向即可保留几乎全部收益，随机方向则失败。共享接口可保留三个独立可加载的KV-Skill且无遗忘。

Conclusion: 任务知识可以从文本或经验中获取，压缩为外部算子，并独立于backbone部署。KV-Skill证明了外部分解式算子作为任务知识存储范式的可行性，兼顾了模块化、独立加载/卸载和有效性。

Abstract: Task knowledge is commonly stored either as text in the prompt or as an update to model weights. Text is modular but must be interpreted on every use, while weight adaptation makes the resulting capability difficult to load, remove, or share independently. We introduce KV-Skill, a design space of external factorized operators that a frozen language model reads through a lightweight interface. KV-Skill supports two complementary paths. Registration converts an authored text skill into a text-derived operator and trains a shared per-backbone interface. Reward learning develops a compact latent operator directly from task outcomes, with or without an authored skill. Neither path adds positions to the prompt. Across ten benchmarks and four backbones from three model families, converting text to a KV-Skill consistently makes the same procedural knowledge more effective. On Qwen3.5-4B LiveMath, registration reaches 77.2 accuracy, compared with 23.4 for the source text skill, 52.0 for SkillOpt, and 64.5 for SoftSkill. Under matched reward training and parameter budgets, KV-Skill gives the best result in seven of eight matched settings against soft prefixes, prefix tuning, and LoRA. A post-hoc rank analysis further shows that text-derived operators retain nearly all of their benefit with one task-aligned direction per injection layer, while matched random directions fail. Finally, one shared interface retains three independently loadable KV-Skills without measurable forgetting. These results show that task knowledge can be acquired from text or experience, compressed into an external operator, and deployed separately from the backbone. Code is available at: https://github.com/shawnzhg/KV-Skill

</details>


### [160] [LC-GRPO: Bridging Train-Inference Gap for Flow-Based GRPO with Langevin Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05600)
*Yingqing Guo,Hui Yuan,Zijian He,Mengdi Wang,Zheng Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LC-GRPO提出了一种带Langevin修正的流式GRPO框架，在每个rollout过渡中先执行与推理对齐的ODE Euler步，再应用Langevin随机修正以匹配边际分布，从而缩小训练时SDE rollouts与测试时ODE采样器之间的分布差距，同时保持各向同性高斯转移核的易处理似然性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式生成模型通常在推理时通过确定性ODE求解采样，而在线强化学习需要随机rollouts进行策略探索与优化。现有GRPO方法在训练时用SDE代替ODE，虽然连续时间下二者边际分布相同，但有限步离散化差异显著——SDE rollouts随着探索噪声增大而变得模糊，导致强化学习训练样本与测试时ODE采样生成的样本之间存在失配。

Method: LC-GRPO的核心创新在于：每个rollout转移先执行一个与推理对齐的ODE Euler步，再施加一个以该时间步边际分布为目标的随机Langevin修正。所需score直接从流速度中恢复，无需额外训练score模型；修正后的转移仍是各向同性高斯分布，具有可处理的似然，便于策略优化。论文还从理论上证明了在适当条件下，一次Langevin修正步能降低不完美ODE Euler步的Wasserstein误差，并且在匹配随机度水平下，所提转移比反向SDE的标准Euler-Maruyama离散化更精确。

Result: 在SD3.5-Medium、FLUX.1-Dev和HunyuanVideo上的实验表明，LC-GRPO在文生图和文生视频任务中持续提升奖励优化效果，保持生成质量，并大幅缩小了随机训练rollouts与确定性测试时ODE推理之间的差距。

Conclusion: LC-GRPO通过将推理对齐的ODE步与Langevin修正相结合，有效解决了流式GRPO训练中SDE离散化与ODE推理采样之间的分布失配问题，兼顾了探索随机性与采样质量，是一种不需要额外score模型且理论上有保障的改进方案。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models are typically sampled by solving a deterministic ordinary differential equation (ODE), whereas online reinforcement learning requires stochastic rollouts for policy exploration and optimization. Existing GRPO methods for flow models therefore replace the inference-time ODE with a stochastic differential equation (SDE) during training. Although the ODE and SDE share the same marginal distributions in continuous time, their finite-step discretizations can differ substantially. In particular, SDE rollouts often become blurry as the exploration noise increases, creating a mismatch between the samples used for reinforcement learning and those generated by the test-time ODE sampler. We introduce LC-GRPO, a flow-based GRPO framework with Langevin correction. Each rollout transition first takes an inference-aligned ODE Euler step and then applies a stochastic Langevin correction targeting the marginal distribution at the resulting timestep. The required score is recovered directly from the flow velocity, requiring no additional score model, while the resulting transition remains an isotropic Gaussian with a tractable likelihood for policy optimization. We theoretically show that, under suitable conditions, one Langevin correction step reduces the Wasserstein error of an imperfect ODE Euler step. At a matched randomness level, we further show that the proposed transition can be more accurate than the standard Euler--Maruyama discretization of the reverse SDE. Experiments on SD3.5-Medium, FLUX.1-Dev, and HunyuanVideo demonstrate that LC-GRPO consistently improves reward optimization across text-to-image and text-to-video tasks, preserves generation quality, and substantially narrows the gap between stochastic training rollouts and deterministic test-time ODE inference.

</details>


### [161] [GAUGE: Granularity-Adaptive Counterfactual Gating of Evidence for Incomplete Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05608)
*Yunping Shi,En Yu,Kairui Guo,Jie Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GAUGE是一个轻量级的反事实门控框架，用于处理不完整多模态分类问题。它通过冻结插补器补全缺失模态，将观测与恢复输入统一编码为细粒度证据单元，利用泰勒证据分数在单次前向-后向传播中计算每个单元的反事实效应，并通过连续门控转化为注意力偏置进行逐单元证据调制，无需改动骨干网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态分类方法在输入不完整时，插补和动态融合机制在粗粒度模态层面操作，无法在同一种被恢复的模态内部保留可靠成分并抑制误导性成分，从而影响预测可靠性。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的插补器补全缺失模态，并将观测与恢复输入统一编码为细粒度证据单元；2) 通过预测感知的泰勒证据分数（单次前向-后向传播）计算每个单元替换为参考表征的反事实效应；3) 将分数映射为连续门控，转换为加性注意力logit偏置，实现逐单元证据调制，无需改动骨干结构。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，GAUGE在各种不完整输入设置下均优于强基线方法。此外，泰勒余项理论分析刻画了一阶近似相对于精确反事实效应的误差界，验证了该框架的理论合理性。

Conclusion: GAUGE为不完整多模态分类下的细粒度证据控制提供了一个原则性且可扩展的框架，兼具理论保障与实证优势。

Abstract: Multimodal classification typically assumes all modalities are available, yet real-world inputs are often incomplete. Imputation and dynamic fusion can mitigate such incompleteness, but existing methods operate at a coarse modality level and thus cannot retain reliable components while suppressing misleading ones within the same recovered modality, compromising prediction reliability. To address this issue, we propose GAUGE, a lightweight counterfactual gating framework for incomplete multimodal classification.
